# f_dud

## FUNCTION public.f_dud()

- 반환: `character varying`
- 줄 수: 1305

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.f_dud()
 RETURNS character varying
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DECLARE
    c01             RECORD;
    c02             RECORD;

    v_APR17         apr17_corp_doc%ROWTYPE;
    v_APR18         apr18_relate_corp_doc%ROWTYPE;

    v_HOLD_QTY      NUMERIC;
    v_HOLD_PUR_AMT  NUMERIC;
    v_HOLD_BOOK_AMT NUMERIC;
    v_PUR_AMT       NUMERIC;
    v_BOOK_AMT      NUMERIC;

    v_RESULT        VARCHAR(1);

    v_seq           NUMERIC;
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BEGIN
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 영임 작업용 Function !!!!
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 전표번호 작업하기
    /*FOR c01 IN ( SELECT act02_company_id, act02_date
                   FROM act02_journal
                  GROUP BY act02_company_id, act02_date
                  ORDER BY act02_company_id, act02_date
    ) LOOP
        v_seq := 0;
        FOR c02 IN ( SELECT *
                       FROM act02_journal
                      WHERE act02_company_id = c01.act02_company_id
                        AND act02_date       = c01.act02_date
                      ORDER BY act02_date, act02_journal_id
        ) LOOP
            -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
            v_seq := v_seq + 1;
            -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
            UPDATE act02_journal
               SET act02_journal_no = v_seq
             WHERE act02_journal_id = c02.act02_journal_id;
            -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
        END LOOP;
    END LOOP;*/
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 펀드보수 연초반제시키기
    /*FOR C01 IN ( SELECT *
                   FROM ast06_tr
                  WHERE ast06_tr_cd = '9910'
                    AND ast06_note = '펀드보수결산'
                    AND ast06_date = '2022-12-31'
                    AND ast06_company_id = 10489
    ) LOOP
        v_RESULT := f_create_back_journal ( C01.ast06_tr_id );
    END LOOP;*/
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 자기매매 보유원장 전체 생성
    /*FOR C01 IN ( SELECT *
                   FROM emp01_person
                  WHERE emp01_company_id = 1
    ) LOOP
        v_RESULT := f_insert_floorTrBook ( C01.emp01_company_id
                                         , C01.emp01_person_id
                                         , '%'
                                         , '%'
                                         , '%'
                                         , '2023-5-1'
                                         );
    END LOOP;*/
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- (3201:금융상품 매도 / 3202:금융상품(RP/CMA) 매도 / 3210:금융상품 상환 / 3301:권리행사(전환사채전환출고))
    -- 거래내역에 차감 취득금액 계산하여 UPDATE
    /*FOR C01 IN ( SELECT ast06_company_id, ast06_date, ast06_ast_class_tree_id, ast08_account_id
                   FROM ast06_tr
                        INNER JOIN ast08_tr_finance ON ast08_tr_id = ast06_tr_id
                  WHERE --ast06_tr_cd IN ('3201','3202','3210','3301','3150')
                        ast08_book_amt IS NOT null
                  GROUP BY ast06_company_id, ast06_date, ast06_ast_class_tree_id, ast08_account_id
                  ORDER BY ast06_company_id, ast06_date
    ) LOOP

        RAISE NOTICE '----------------------------------------------------------------';
        RAISE NOTICE '회사ID[%] - 거래일[%] - 거래종목[%] - 보관계좌[%]', C01.ast06_company_id, C01.ast06_date, C01.ast06_ast_class_tree_id, C01.ast08_account_id;

        ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 거래전일 수량,장부금액,취득금액 GET
        SELECT ast09_hold_qty
             , ast09_pur_amt
             , ast09_book_amt
          INTO v_HOLD_QTY
             , v_HOLD_PUR_AMT
             , v_HOLD_BOOK_AMT
          FROM (
                 SELECT ast09_hold_date
                      , ast09_hold_qty
                      , ast09_pur_amt
                      , CASE WHEN f_get_account_close_date(ast09_company_id, ast09_hold_date, 0) = ast09_hold_date
                             THEN ast09_eval_amt
                             ELSE ast09_book_amt END    ast09_book_amt
                   FROM ast09_book
                        INNER JOIN ast01_class_tree ON ast01_class_tree_id = ast09_ast_class_tree_id
                  WHERE ast09_company_id         = C01.ast06_company_id
                    AND ast09_ast_class_tree_id  = C01.ast06_ast_class_tree_id
                    AND ast09_keep_account_id    = C01.ast08_account_id
                    AND ast09_hold_date          < C01.ast06_date
                    AND NOT EXISTS ( SELECT 1
                                       FROM ast11_book_close
                                      WHERE ast11_company_id        = ast09_company_id
                                        AND ast11_ast_class_tree_id = ast09_ast_class_tree_id
                                        AND ast11_keep_account_id   = ast09_keep_account_id
                                        AND ast11_hold_date         = ast09_hold_date )
                  UNION ALL
                 SELECT ast11_hold_date
                      , ast11_hold_qty
                      , ast11_pur_amt
                      , ast11_book_amt
                   FROM ast11_book_close
                  WHERE ast11_company_id         = C01.ast06_company_id
                    AND ast11_ast_class_tree_id  = C01.ast06_ast_class_tree_id
                    AND ast11_keep_account_id    = C01.ast08_account_id
                    AND ast11_hold_date          < C01.ast06_date
                  ORDER BY ast09_hold_date DESC
                  LIMIT 1
               ) t;

        ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 거래일의 거래내역
        FOR C02 IN ( SELECT *
                       FROM ast06_tr
                            INNER JOIN ast08_tr_finance ON ast08_tr_id = ast06_tr_id
                      WHERE ast06_company_id        = C01.ast06_company_id
                        AND ast06_date              = C01.ast06_date
                        AND ast06_ast_class_tree_id = C01.ast06_ast_class_tree_id
                        AND ast08_account_id        = C01.ast08_account_id
                      ORDER BY ast06_tr_id
        ) LOOP
            -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
            -- 당일 매수거래 반영
            IF C02.ast06_tr_cd IN ('3302'   --권리행사(전환주식입고)
                                  ,'3101'   --금융상품 매수
                                  ,'3103'   --금융상품 입고
                                  ,'3503'   --일반공모주청약-청약입고
                                  ) THEN
            
                RAISE NOTICE '    (+) 매수반영 : 거래[%] / 거래전 보유취득금액[%] / 거래전 보유수량[%] / 매수수량[%] / 장부&취득금액[%] / 실제장부금액[%]'
                              , C02.ast06_tr_cd, v_HOLD_PUR_AMT, v_HOLD_QTY, C02.ast08_qty, (C02.ast08_tr_amt - COALESCE(C02.ast08_acr_int_amt,0)), C02.ast08_book_amt;

                v_HOLD_QTY      := COALESCE(v_HOLD_QTY     ,0) + C02.ast08_qty;
                v_HOLD_PUR_AMT  := COALESCE(v_HOLD_PUR_AMT ,0) + C02.ast08_tr_amt - COALESCE(C02.ast08_acr_int_amt,0);
                v_HOLD_BOOK_AMT := COALESCE(v_HOLD_BOOK_AMT,0) + C02.ast08_tr_amt - COALESCE(C02.ast08_acr_int_amt,0);

                UPDATE ast08_tr_finance
                   SET ast08_pur_amt = ast08_book_amt
                 WHERE ast08_tr_finance_id = C02.ast08_tr_finance_id;

            -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
            -- 당일 매도거래 반영
            ELSIF C02.ast06_tr_cd IN ('3201'    --금융상품 매도
                                     ,'3202'    --금융상품(RP/CMA) 매도
                                     ,'3210'    --금융상품 상환
                                     ,'3301'    --권리행사(전환사채전환출고)
                                     ) THEN

                v_PUR_AMT  := trunc(v_HOLD_PUR_AMT  * (C02.ast08_qty / v_HOLD_QTY));
                v_BOOK_AMT := trunc(v_HOLD_BOOK_AMT * (C02.ast08_qty / v_HOLD_QTY));

                RAISE NOTICE '    (-) 매도반영 : 거래[%] / 거래전 보유수량[%] / 매도수량[%]  -  거래전 취득금액[%] / 차감취득금액[%]  -  차감장부금액[%] / 실제차감장부금액[%]'
                              , C02.ast06_tr_cd, v_HOLD_QTY, C02.ast08_qty, v_HOLD_PUR_AMT, v_PUR_AMT, v_BOOK_AMT, C02.ast08_book_amt;

                UPDATE ast08_tr_finance
                   SET ast08_pur_amt = v_PUR_AMT
                 WHERE ast08_tr_finance_id = C02.ast08_tr_finance_id;

                v_HOLD_QTY      := COALESCE(v_HOLD_QTY     ,0) - C02.ast08_qty;
                v_HOLD_PUR_AMT  := COALESCE(v_HOLD_PUR_AMT ,0) - v_PUR_AMT;
                v_HOLD_BOOK_AMT := COALESCE(v_HOLD_BOOK_AMT,0) - v_BOOK_AMT;

            -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
            -- 그밖의 거래 Skip
            ELSE
                RAISE NOTICE '    ( ) 그밖의 거래 SKIP : 거래[%]', C02.ast06_tr_cd;
                CONTINUE;
            END IF;
        END LOOP;
    END LOOP;*/
    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 마일스톤 영업거래처 데이터 등록 (2023-12-06)

    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 공지번호 순서대로 만들기
    FOR C01 IN ( SELECT *
                   FROM bbs02_notice
                  order by bbs02_notice_id
    ) LOOP
        ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        UPDATE bbs02_notice
           SET bbs02_notice_no = f_create_notice_no(bbs02_company_id)
         WHERE bbs02_notice_id = C01.bbs02_notice_id;
    END LOOP;

    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    RETURN 'Y';

        RETURN v_errMsg||'-'||SQLERRM;*/
        RETURN SQLERRM;
END;
```
